### Fast convolution and score
```
def next_pow2(n): return 1 << (n-1).bit_length()
def fast_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=len(a)
    size=2*n-1
    fftsize=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, fftsize)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, fftsize)[:size]
    # remove tiny negative? c=np.maximum(c,0)
    return c
def score_fft(a):
    c=fast_conv(a)
    return 2.0*len(a)*float(c.max()), float(c.max()), c
```
Potential FFT roundoff can give max slightly above? Nonnegative exact. Use `np.errstate`.

### Normalization
```
def normalize_pos(a, eps=1e-30):
   a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,float),0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=eps: return np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
   a=a/s
   # optionally floor: a=np.maximum(a, 1e-300) ; renorm
   return a
```
For mirror, keep positive: a = np.maximum(a, tiny) with tiny=1e-300? log of 1e-300 okay (-690). But multiply may underflow. Use tiny=1e-300/n? Fine. If a has zeros, floor changes sum tiny. We can set floor=1e-15/n? That may disrupt (n*1e-15=1e-11, negligible score 2n*(1e-11)^2? negligible). Use floor=1e-15 maybe each coordinate too high for n large? If n=30000, 1e-15*30000=3e-11, sum perturbation 3e-11, fine. Score effect ~? no. Use small. Or keep exact positive by initial no zeros and mirror keeps positive. If coord peel sets zeros? We can avoid zeros. Final can leave positive floor.

def mirror_optimize(a0, time_limit=60, K=200, beta=0.0, step_sizes=(0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.8,1.0), verbose=True):
    a=normalize_pos(a0)
    n=len(a)
    full_len=2*n-1
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_fft(a)[0]
    t0=time.monotonic()
    stall=0
    beta_cycle_idx=0
    for it in range(1, maxit):
       if time.monotonic()-t0>time_limit: break
       c=fast_conv(a)
       M=c.max()
       sc=2*n*M
       if sc<best_score-1e-12: best=a.copy(); best_score=sc; stall=0
       else: stall+=1
       if stall>stall_limit: break/restart
       # weights
       top = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
       # use temperature based on gap M-c[top[-1]]
       top_c=c[top]
       # If beta=0 use penalty = M - min_c_top maybe; else
       if beta>0:
           w=np.exp(beta*(top_c - M)); w/=w.sum()
       else:
           gap = max(1e-15, M - top_c[-1])
           w=np.exp((top_c-top_c[0])/(gap)); w/=w.sum()
       # compute gradient using only top weights
       G=fft_gradient_top(a, top, w)  # returns 2 sum w_k a_{k-i}? (weights normalized sum1? Actually gradient of sum w c_k. If weights sum1, G components ~2/n.
       # normalize
       G_mean=G.mean()
       Grange=G.max()-G.min()
       if Grange < 1e-300: no update
       u=(G-Gmean)/Grange
       # try steps
       best_step=0? best_trial=best_score? We want reduce sc from current? Use current sc as base; maybe keep if not improve? Mirror local. We can accept if new_sc < current or best? To avoid oscillation, require < current_sc - tol? But might get stuck; accept if < best. For nonconvex, may backtrack.
       base_log=np.log(a+eps)
       for step in step_sizes:
           z=base_log - step*u
           z -= z.max()
           newa=np.exp(z)
           newa=np.maximum(newa, floor)
           newa/=newa.sum()
           newsc=score_fft(newa)[0]
           if newsc < best_trial: ...
       if no improvement: maybe use aggressive step or random perturbation
```

### Coordinate polish

Let's design `chu_estep`:
```
def additive_mirror_step(a, G, step):
    u = G - G.mean()
    new = a - step*u
    new = np.maximum(new, floor)
    new/=new.sum()
```
Since G normalized to range 1, step maybe 1e-3? a~1/n, so step must relative: step = alpha * (a.mean()) / range? If u normalized by range, step alpha * a.mean()? Use `step = alpha / n` maybe. Mirror multiplicative with u normalized and step 0.1 changes by 10%, likely good. We can use both and choose best.

#### Mirror optimizer details
```
def mirror_optimize(a0, time_limit, max_iter=1000000, K0=30, Kmax=500, step_values=(0.05,0.15,0.3,0.6,1.0,1.5), floor=1e-15, verbose=True):
    a=normalize_pos(a0, floor)
    n=len(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_fft(a)[0]
    t0=time.monotonic(); stall=0; it=0
    K=K0; best_no_improve_long=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0<time_limit:
       it+=1
       c=fast_conv(a)
       M=c.max(); sc=2*n*M
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_sc=sc; best=a.copy(); stall=0
       else:
           stall+=1
       # adapt K: start small, grow, then if stalled reset
       target_K = min(Kmax, K0 + it//2)
       if stall > 300: target_K = min(Kmax, K0 + stall//5)
       if stall > 1500: break
       # weights top target_K
       K_real = min(target_K, len(c))
       order=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K_real]
       topc=c[order]
       # choice of scale: if M-topc[-1] >0 use relative
       gap = max(M - topc[-1], M*0.05, 1e-300)
       # Use exp((topc - M)/gap) (negative) weights; sum
       w = np.exp((topc - M)/gap)
       ws=w.sum()
       if ws <=0 or not finite: w = np.ones(K_real)/K_real
       else: w/=ws
       # To avoid too many effective, maybe sharpen: if stall large, use smaller gap
       if stall>500: gap *=0.5
       # gradient via fft with w
       G=fft_gradient_weights(a, order, w)
       if not finite: continue
       Gmean=G.mean(); Grange=G.max()-G.min()
       if Grange < 1e-18:
           # random perturb
           ...
           continue
       u=(G-Gmean)/Grange
       # line search steps
       base=np.log(a+floor)
       cur_best_sc=sc; cur_a=a
       # try steps maybe sorted by expected
       for step in step_values:
           z=base - step*u
           z -= z.max()
           cand=np.exp(z)
           # If cand sum underflow? z max subtract so max 1, exp others <=1; sum >=1. Good.
           cand=np.maximum(cand, 0.0) # if underflow to 0 okay? But zero floor? We can allow zeros? If zeros, log next issues. Add floor after?
           cand += floor
           cand/=cand.sum()
           cand_sc=score_fft(cand)[0]
           if cand_sc < cur_best_sc - 1e-14:
               cur_best_sc=cand_sc; cur_a=cand
       if cur_sc < sc - 1e-13 or it < 20:
           a=cur_a
       else:
           # no acceptable; try more aggressive: use step with smallest candidate maybe if <best_sc?
           if cur_best_sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
               a=cur_a
           else:
               # perturb around best
               if stall > 50:
                  a = normalize_pos(best + 0.01*np.random.default_rng(it).standard_normal(n)*best)
                  continue
       if it%200==0: print
    return best,best_sc
```
But note line search uses `cur_best_sc=sc`, so if no candidate lower than current, we keep a. The best_sc may be unchanged. If a worsened, revert. This is strictly descent. Strict descent in nonconvex could stall. We can allow accepting if candidate has lower score than best (could be current same) but if current higher than candidate? We always update if any candidate < current. If none, perturb.

Maybe use **best-neighbor** but for mirror with normalized gradient, small step likely improves. Good.

Potential issue: `base=np.log(a+floor)` each iteration O(n) and uses floor; okay. Adding floor after exp may bias. We can avoid floor in base by ensuring a>0. If a has zeros, set a=min floor. At end, zeros can be set to 0? Keep positive floor.

We should use `np.exp` with z minus max; if u range large and step large, some z very negative -> underflow to 0; after adding floor, okay. Sum of cand after floor maybe floor*n + exps. If many zeros, sum floor*n ~ n*1e-15=~2e-11, negligible relative 1? Wait cand before floor sum after exp max subtract = at least 1 (one coordinate 1). Adding floor and renorm barely changes. Good.

#### Gradient function with top weights
```
def fft_gradient_weights(a, active, w):
    n=len(a)
    L=2*n-1
    W=np.zeros(L)
    W[active]=w
    Fl=W[::-1]
    fft_size=1 << (3*n-3).bit_length()  # length n+L-1 =3n-2
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    B=np.fft.rfft(Fl, fft_size)
    R=np.fft.irfft(A*B, fft_size)
    return R[2*n-2:n-2:-1]
```
Need verify fft_size: conv length n + (L-1)=n+2n-2=3n-2? Wait W length L=2n-1, linear convolution length n+L-1 = n+2n-2=3n-2. Next pow >=3n-2. `3*n-3`? If size=3n-2, bit_length of size-1 =3n-3. Yes.
Slice: R indices from 2n-2 down to n-1 inclusive length n. Previous used `2*n-2 : n-2 : -1` which stops at n-1. Good.
Return not multiplied by 2.
Potential memory: W full zeros each iter length up to 60000, fine. For n=25000, 3n-2=74998, next pow 131072. FFT bigger. Maybe cap n to 20000 to use 65536? 3*20000-2=59998, next pow 65536. Good. For 25000, 131072 (2x cost). Cap 20000. Let's cap max optimization length 20000. Parent 11700, interpolate to 20000. Good. Final eval direct 400M okay.

Could compute gradient direct for top active to avoid 65536? For K up to 500 and n=20000, direct 10M ops, maybe more. FFT fine.

#### Coordinate polish details
We need a function that uses current best and active lags.
```
def coord_polish(a0, time_limit, top_K=80, cand_src=8, cand_dst=20, fractions=(0.01,0.03,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5), verbose=True):
    a=normalize_pos(a0); n=len(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_fft(a)[0]; t0=time.monotonic(); stall=0; rng=np.random.default_rng(1234)
    while time < limit and stall < 2000:
        c=fast_conv(a); M=c.max(); sc=2*n*M
        update best/stall
        order=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_K]
        # weights maybe uniform or softmax; pressure = gradient/2 with soft all top? Use weights uniform top? To identify pressure.
        G=fft_gradient_weights(a, order, np.ones(len(order))/len(order))
        # high pressure indices: top 30 (excluding near zero)
        high=np.argsort(G)[::-1][:cand_src]
        low=np.argsort(G)[:cand_dst]
        # Also random low/high add
        improved=False
        # Precompute? For each high, for each low, for fractions, do move and score
        # To reduce cost, pick some random pairs
        pair_candidates=[]
        for hi in high[:cand_src]:
           if a[hi]<1e-18: continue
           for lo in low[:cand_dst]:
              if lo!=hi: pair_candidates.append((hi,lo))
        # maybe truncate to 50 pairs
        np.random.shuffle? Can't top-level closure. Use rng.permutation
        for hi,lo in pair_candidates[:40]:
            if time up: break
            amt_max=a[hi]
            # try fractions, choose best step via quadratic? just fractions sorted descending? try large/small
            best_pair_sc=sc; best_pair_a=None
            for frac in fractions:
                amt=amt_max*frac
                if amt<1e-18: continue
                cand=a.copy(); cand[hi]-=amt; cand[lo]+=amt
                cand=np.maximum(cand,0); s=cand.sum(); if s<=0 continue; cand/=s
                cand_sc=score_fft(cand)[0]
                if cand_sc < best_pair_sc - 1e-14:
                    best_pair_sc=cand_sc; best_pair_a=cand
            if best_pair_a is not None:
                a=best_pair_a; improved=True; stall=0
                break # recompute
        if not improved:
            stall+=1
            if stall>200:
                # random swaps
                i=rng.integers(n); j=rng.integers(n)
                if i!=j and a[i]>0:
                   frac=rng.uniform(0.02,0.2)
                   amt=a[i]*frac
                   cand=a.copy(); cand[i]-=amt; cand[j]+=amt; ...
                   if improve: ...
    return best,best_sc
```
This does up to 40*7=280 FFTs per accepted improvement iteration. If many improvements, time. Limit by time. Maybe 20 pairs*5 fractions=100. Use adaptive.

We can improve by using **active-set line search** without full conv for each fraction: For hi->lo, the change in c vector can be computed O(n) by updating c from base? We have base c. For a move δ, Δc as derived. Then max can be computed by c + linear_shifts + impulses. For each pair, we can compute candidates δ quickly O(n) without FFT. This is much faster and allows many more. Let's implement.

Given base c (length L=2n-1), base a. Move i->j: δ. Let dc = δ (e_j - e_i). cδ = c + 2δ (x*e_j - x*e_i) + δ² [(e_j*e_j - e_j*e_i - e_i*e_j + e_i*e_i)]. The linear terms: vector left = δ * shift of a by j minus shift by i. Specifically `lin = 2*( delta * (a shifted to index j) - delta * (a shifted to index i) )`, where shift of a by p: v[p]=a[0]? For x*e_p, index k: a_{k-p}. So valid k in [p,p+n-1] with value a_{k-p}. Add to c. Quadratic impulses:
- +δ² at k=2j (e_j*e_j)
- -2δ² at k=j+i (if j≠i; if j=i none, but move i≠j)
- +δ² at k=2i.
But wait dc=δ(e_j - e_i); dc*dc = δ²(e_j*e_j - e_j*e_i - e_i*e_j + e_i*e_i). Since convolution commutative, e_j*e_i and e_i*e_j same index i+j. So impulse at i+j with -2δ². Good.
For each δ, we can compute modified max by applying linear shifts (two shifted arrays) and impulses. But doing vector full length for each δ O(L). We can precompute base arrays for pair: lin_pos = 2a shifted by j, lin_neg = 2a shifted by i. For each δ: c_mod = c + δ(lin_pos - lin_neg) + impulses. Need max. Vector operation O(L) per δ. L~30000, fractions 7 =>210k ops, for 40 pairs 8M, in numpy C fast but creating arrays each time. Better than FFT? Maybe comparable. Could use direct loop over several δ by vectorizing across δ: shape (L, nfracs). But memory. For polish, FFT exact simpler. But O(n) per fraction may be faster than FFT and exact (up to rounding). Let's implement polynomial move evaluation to allow more candidates.

Function `score_move_from_c(a, c, i, j, deltas)`:
- L=2n-1.
- Create `diff = np.zeros(L)`? For each pair, we can compute two shift arrays. Instead of full `c_mod` for each delta, we can compute base max? Need max over k of c[k] + delta*(P[k]-N[k]) + impulses. For a set of deltas, we can vectorize:
```
P=np.zeros(L); N=np.zeros(L)
# shift j:
start_j=j; end_j=j+n-1 within L
P[start_j:end_j] = 2*a  # because k-j=i, a_i
start_i=i; end_i=i+n-1
N[start_i:end_i] = 2*a
linear = P - N  # length L
D = np.array(fractions)*a[i]? absolute deltas
# For each delta d, iq=i+j, iq2j=2j, iq2i=2i
# c_plus = c + d*linear
# max = max(c_plus) and adjust impulses: c_plus[q] += ...
# Since impulses at few indices, max could be at impulse. We can compute max_base = c_plus.max(); then value_at_q = c_plus[q]+impulse; return max(max_base, values)
```
Need be careful: Linear update formula uses original a including a_i unchanged? When moving mass, a changes; the linear cross terms with original a are correct first-order? Wait exact c for new a_i'=a_i-d, a_j'=a_j+d. Use expansion:
Original C = A*A.
New = (A + d(e_j-e_i))*...
Linear = 2d (A*e_j - A*e_i) where A is original a. This includes terms with a_i and a_j but they will be adjusted by quadratic impulses. Let's verify for small n. a=[a0,a1], move 0->1 d: original c0=a0², c1=2a0a1,c2=a1². New b0=a1+d? Actually a0'=a0-d,a1'=a1+d. New c0=(a0-d)²=a0²-2a0d+d². Formula: linear P e1: 2d a shifted1: P at k=1:2a0; at2:2a1. N e0: at0:2a0; at1:2a1. linear at0=-2a0d, at1=2a0d-2a1d, at2=2a1d. Quadratic: +d² at2i=0; -2d² at i+j=1; +d² at2j=2. c0: -2a0d+d² correct. c1: 2a0d-2a1d-2d² =2d(a0-a1-d) but actual c1=2(a0-d)(a1+d)=2a0a1+2d(a0-a1)-2d². original c1=2a0a1, yes. c2: +2a1d+d² correct. Good.

We can evaluate multiple deltas for a pair with vectorized arrays. But for many pairs, constructing P,N each pair O(L) adds cost. We can precompute shifts via direct for each i? Too much. For a pair, use slice assignment.

Polish algorithm with O(L) per delta may consume memory/time. We can just use full FFT for selected few moves; simpler. But O(n) exact for many can improve. Let's create a function `try_move_fft` maybe simpler.

```
def mirror_optimize(a0, time_limit, K0=50, Kmax=500, max_iter=1000000, verbose=True):
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return a, inf
    c=fast_convolution(a); M=c.max(); best_sc=2.0*n*M; best=a.copy()
    t0=time.monotonic(); stall=0; it=0
    floor=1e-15
    step_list = [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.55,0.8,1.2]
    # We can precompute? no.
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1
        if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
        c=fast_convolution(a)
        M=float(c.max())
        sc=2*n*M
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc=sc; best=a.copy(); stall=0
        else:
            stall += 1
        if stall > 3000: break
        # active weights
        K = min(Kmax, K0 + it//5)
        if stall > 400:
            K = max(10, K//2)  # sharpen?
        if K > len(c): K=len(c)
        # Get top K indices
        # np.argsort on 23k each iteration may be O(n log n) slow. Use nsmallest? For K small, `np.argpartition(c, K-1)[:K]` then sort top maybe. Need top order for weights; c not needed sorted. Use argpartition to get unsorted top K, then c top. For gradient weights order no need sorted. For gap, max/min top.
        if K == len(c):
            idx = np.arange(len(c))
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, K-1)[:K]
        topc = c[idx]
        gap = max(M - float(topc.min()), M*0.1, 1e-300)
        # sharpen: if K small, gap small; if stall, smaller
        if stall > 500: gap *= 0.5
        # weights exp((topc - M)/gap)
        ex = np.exp((topc - M)/gap)  # topc<=M
        sw=ex.sum()
        if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw<=0:
           w=np.ones(K)/K
        else:
           w=ex/sw
        # To avoid too wide, maybe apply power: w=(w**2)/sum? optional not.
        G=fft_gradient_weights(a, idx, w)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)): continue
        gmean=float(G.mean()); gmin=float(G.min()); gmax=float(G.max()); grange=gmax-gmean
        if grange < 1e-300:
            # perturb
            break? no, random
            a = perturb...
            stall=0
            continue
        u=(G-gmean)/grange
        # line search. To reduce FFTs, first try two steps 0.3 and 0.8; if one improves, maybe try around.
        base_log = np.log(a + floor)
        best_trial = sc
        best_trial_a = None
        for step in step_list:
            z = base_log - step * u
            z -= z.max()
            cand = np.exp(z)
            cand += floor
            ssum = cand.sum()
            if not finite or ssum<=0: continue
            cand /= ssum
            tmp_sc = score_fft(cand)[0]
            if tmp_sc < best_trial - 1e-14:
                best_trial = tmp_sc; best_trial_a = cand
        if best_trial_a is not None:
            a = best_trial_a
            if best_trial < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc=best_trial; best=a.copy(); stall=0
            else:
                # improved current but not best? impossible if best_sc<=sc? best_sc maybe >? If a not best. Could happen. Revert to best? Better keep current if descent; may compose later. But if not close best, revert to best occasionally.
                if best_trial > best_sc:
                    # revert to best? strict objective decreased from current but best is lower; keep a? It might be in different basin? If current sc > best_sc and trial > best_sc, no reason.
                    a = best.copy()
                # stall? no improvement global
                stall = max(0, stall)
        else:
            # no local step; perturb from best if stalls
            if stall > 100:
                rng = np.random.default_rng(12345 + stall)
                a = random_perturb(best, rng, rel_std=0.02 + 0.005*(stall//1000))
                stall=0
            else:
                # try step 2.0 strongly
                z=base_log - 2.0*u; ... maybe
        if verbose and it % 200 ==0: print
    return best, best_sc
```

### Random perturb function
```
def random_perturb(a, rng, rel_std=0.03):
    b = a + rng.standard_normal(len(a)) * rel_std * a
    # also add tiny uniform to zeros? 
    b += 1e-12
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
    s=b.sum()
    if s<=0: return a.copy()
    return b/s
```

Implement `fast_convolution(a)`:
```
def fast_convolution(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.array([0.0])
    size = 2*n - 1
    fft_size = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    C = np.fft.irfft(Fa * Fa, fft_size)[:size]
    # Clean tiny imaginary? C real.
    return C
```
No `a` list conversion issues.

`score_fft(a)`:
```

```

```

### LP-FW implementation details

`get_solver`:
```
def get_solver():
    global _cp_solver
    if _cp_solver is not None: return _cp_solver
    if not _HAS_CP: return None
    for s in [cp.CLARABEL, cp.PDLP, cp.SCS]:
       try:
          x=cp.Variable(10, nonneg=True); prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)),[cp.sum(x)==1]); prob.solve(solver=s, verbose=False); if status optimal: _cp_solver=s; return s
       except: pass
    return None
```
No ECOS? Can include ECOS. But CLARABLE maybe. `cp.Problem` small.

### Let's flesh coordinate polish without lambda
```
def coordinate_polish(...):
  ...
  high_count=12; low_count=24
  ...
  idx = np.argpartition(c, K-1)[:K]
  topc=c[idx]; gap=...; w=...; w/=w.sum()
  G=fft_gradient_weights(a, idx, w)
  high_all = np.argsort(G)[::-1][:high_count*3]
  low_all = np.argsort(G)[:low_count*2]
  # filter nonnegative a
  high=[]
  for h in high_all:
     if a[h] > 1e-16:
        high.append(int(h))
        if len(high)>high_count: break
  low=[]
  for l in low_all:
     if int(l) not in high_set? Use set? small. We can create high_set = set(high)
     if l not in high_set:
        low.append(int(l)); if len>low_count break
  # pairs loop top high_count, low_count, max pairs param
  max_pairs = 16
  count=0
  improved_flag=False
  for hi in high[:high_count]:
     if count>=max_pairs: break
     amt=a[hi]
     if amt <= 1e-16: continue
     for lo in low[:low_count]:
        if count>=max_pairs: break
        if lo==hi: continue
        # try fractions
        best_pair_sc=sc
        best_pair_a=None
        for frac in fractions:
            delta=amt*frac
            cand=a.copy()
            cand[hi]-=delta
            if cand[hi] < -1e-15: # too large? frac <=1 so no
            cand[lo]+=delta
            cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
            s=cand.sum(); if s<=0: continue
            cand/=s
            cand_sc=score_fft(cand)[0]
            if cand_sc < best_pair_sc - 1e-14:
                best_pair_sc=cand_sc; best_pair_a=cand
        if best_pair_a is not None:
            a=best_pair_a; improved_flag=True; stall=0; break
        count +=1
  if improved_flag: continue
```
But this evaluates up to max_pairs*len(fractions) =16*5=80 FFTs per round. Good. No sort. High and low in pressure order; pairs high x low. Good.

### Potential issue: Mirror update `z=base_log - step*u`. `base_log` for a zero = log(floor) ~ -34 (floor 1e-15). We can set initial floor by `a=np.maximum(a,1e-15)` then renorm. In `normalize_sequence` with floor optional? We'll add in mirror: `a=np.maximum(a, 1e-15); a/=a.sum()`.

### Coordinate polish floor: It may set a[hi] zero after move if frac=1? Fractions <=0.5 so not zero. Good. Random moves not zero if frac <1.

### LP-FW: y normalized floor? Use `normalize_sequence` floor 1e-30; LP variables nonnegative; line search may create zeros? We can floor after update: `trial=np.maximum(trial,1e-15); trial/=sum`.

### Pick best sequence at phase: Use a helper? Need no function returning tuple? We can do inline. Maybe define `update_best`? Top-level could return new best? But best variables local; no lambda. We can just repeated.

### Potential issue if `budget_s` not passed and is 1800 default. Good.

### Let's think of using `random` module? Use np rng.

### Code style: Avoid comments too lengthy? Fine.

_FFT_GRAD_CACHE={}

def get_fft_conv_size(n):
    v=_FFT_CONV_CACHE.get(n)
    if v is None:
       size=2*n-1
       v=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
       _FFT_CONV_CACHE[n]=v
    return v
```

`fast_convolution`:
```
def fast_convolution(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==1:
       return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
    fft_size=get_fft_conv_size(n)
    Fa=np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fa, fft_size)[:2*n-1]
    return c
```
For n=1 works general too but okay.

`fast_cross_convolution`:
```
if n==1: return [a0*b0]
else similar.
```

`score_fft` returns float. If c.max() maybe negative due roundoff? For zero? If all zeros, max 0. Use float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0. If m<0 and >-1e-15, set 0.

a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
if a.size==0: return np.array([],float)
a=np.clip(a,0.0,1e300)
s=a.sum()
if not finite or s<=1e-300: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
a=a/s
if floor>0:
   a=np.maximum(a,floor)
   a=a/a.sum()
return a
```
Clipping upper 1e300 not needed; evaluator clips 1000 but search values small. If parent has huge >1000, normalize to sum1 makes small; no issue. But if we return sum1, no clipping. Good.

`make_initial_templates(n)`:
```
def make_initial_templates(n):
    out=[]
    x=(np.arange(n,dtype=float)+0.5)/n
    out.append(np.ones(n))
    for p in (-0.5,-0.35,-0.25,-0.15,0.0,0.15,0.3,0.5,1.0):
       out.append((x+1e-12)**p)
    # tapered singular
    out.append((x+1e-12)**-0.5 * (1.0 - 0.3*x))
    # two endpoint blocks
    L=max(1,n//3)
    b=np.zeros(n); b[:L]=1; b[-L:]=1; out.append(b)
    # maybe block lengths 0.45
    L=max(1,int(n*0.45)); b=np.zeros(n); b[:L]=1; b[-L:]=1; out.append(b)
    # mix singular + bump
    for cpos in (0.25,0.5,0.75):
       b=(x+1e-12)**-0.5 + 0.5*np.exp(-((x-cpos)**2)/(2*0.05))
       out.append(b)
    res=[]
    for s in out:
       s=normalize_sequence(s)
       if s is not None and s.size==n and np.isfinite(s).all(): res.append(s)
    return res
```
Scoring 20 templates * FFT maybe okay. If n=20k, 20 FFTs. Good.

### `fft_gradient_weights` correctness with cached size
```
def fft_gradient_weights(a, active, weights):
    n=len(a)
    if n<=1: return np.zeros_like(a)
    L=2*n-1
    W=np.zeros(L,dtype=np.float64)
    # active may be array int, weights float
    W[active]=weights
    Fl=W[::-1]
    fft_size=get_fft_grad_size(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    B=np.fft.rfft(Fl, fft_size)
    R=np.fft.irfft(A*B, fft_size)
    return R[2*n-2:n-2:-1]
```
Need ensure active indices within 0..L-1. They are from c length L. weights sum maybe. If active empty? return zeros.

`get_fft_grad_size(n)`:
```
size=3*n-2
v = 1 << (size-1).bit_length()
```
For n=1, size1; okay.

### `mirror_optimize` detailed code with no closures
```
def mirror_optimize(a0, time_limit, K0=40, Kmax=400, verbose=True):
    a=normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a.size==0 or time_limit<=0: return a, score_fft(a)
    a=np.maximum(a, 1e-15); a=a/a.sum()
    n=a.size
    c=fast_convolution(a); M=float(c.max()); best_sc=2*n*M; best=a.copy()
    t0=time.monotonic(); stall=0; it=0
    step_list=(0.2,0.5,1.0,1.8)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        it+=1
        c=fast_convolution(a)
        M=float(c.max()); sc=2*n*M
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc=sc; best=a.copy(); stall=0
        else:
            stall+=1
        if stall>4000: break
        L=2*n-1
        target_K = min(Kmax, K0 + it//10)
        if stall > 500:
            target_K = max(20, min(target_K, 120))
        if stall > 1500:
            target_K = 20
        K = min(target_K, L)
        if K <= 0: break
        if K == L:
            idx=np.arange(L)
        else:
            idx=np.argpartition(c, K-1)[:K]
        topc=c[idx]
        tmin=float(topc.min()) if topc.size else M
        gap=max(M - tmin, M*0.05, 1e-300)
        if stall > 400:
            gap *= 0.6
        ex=np.exp((topc - M)/gap)
        sw=ex.sum()
        if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0:
            w=np.ones(K)/K
        else:
            w=ex/sw
        G=fft_gradient_weights(a, idx, w)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
            a=best.copy(); stall=0; continue
        gmean=float(G.mean()); gmin=float(G.min()); gmax=float(G.max()); grange=gmax-gmean
        if grange < 1e-300:
            # random perturb if stalled
            if stall > 50:
                rng=np.random.default_rng(98765+stall)
                a=random_perturb(best, rng, 0.02)
                stall=0
            else:
                break
            continue
        u=(G-gmean)/grange
        base_log=np.log(a + 1e-300)
        best_trial=sc
        best_trial_a=None
        for step in step_list:
            z=base_log - step*u
            z -= z.max()
            cand=np.exp(z)
            cand += 1e-15
            ssum=cand.sum()
            if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0:
                continue
            cand /= ssum
            trial_sc=score_fft(cand)[0]
            if trial_sc < best_trial - 1e-14:
                best_trial=trial_sc; best_trial_a=cand
        if best_trial_a is not None:
            if best_trial < sc - 1e-13:
                a=best_trial_a
                if best_trial < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc=best_trial; best=a.copy(); stall=0
                else:
                    stall = max(stall, 0)
            else:
                a=best.copy()
                if stall > 100:
                    rng=np.random.default_rng(11111+stall)
                    a=random_perturb(best, rng, 0.02 + 0.01*(stall//1000))
                    stall=0
        else:
            a=best.copy()
            if stall > 80:
                rng=np.random.default_rng(22222+stall)
                a=random_perturb(best, rng, 0.02 + 0.01*(stall//1000))
                stall=0
        if verbose and it % 250 == 0:
            print("  mirror it={} t={:.1f}s score={:.7f} best={:.7f} stall={}".format(it, time.monotonic()-t0, sc, best_sc, stall))
    return best, best_sc
```
One issue: If current a=best and no candidate improves current, we set a=best and perturb. This may repeatedly perturb with same rng seed and fail. Seed varies with stall. Rel_std increases but capped? Could oscillate. Limit stall 4000, then break.

Maybe use **accepting candidates that improve best even if not current** is impossible if current==best. Good.

`random_perturb` top-level:
```
def random_perturb(a, rng, rel_std=0.02):
    b=a + rng.standard_normal(a.size)*rel_std*a
    b=b + 1e-15
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    s=b.sum()
    if s<=0: return a.copy()
    return b/s
```

### `score_fft` returns tuple? In above we use `score_fft(cand)[0]` but defined maybe returns only float. Let's define `score_fft(a)` returns float; and `score_and_conv(a)`? Simpler: `score_fft` returns float. In mirror we need c for current separately. For candidate, `score_fft(cand)`. Good. Adjust.

`score_fft`:
```
c=fast_convolution(a); m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0; if m<0: m=0; return 2*len(a)*m
```

### `coordinate_polish` with `score_fft` float.

```
def coordinate_polish(a0,time_limit,high_count=10,low_count=20,max_pairs=16,verbose=False):
    a=normalize_sequence(a0); if size 0 return
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-15); a/=sum
    n=a.size; best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_fft(a); t0=...; stall=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(424242)
    fractions=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6)
    while time < limit:
       sc=score_fft(a)
       if sc < best_sc -1e-12: best...
       else stall++
       if stall>3000: break
       c=fast_convolution(a); M=...
       K=min(80,2*n-1)
       idx=np.argpartition(c,K-1)[:K]
       topc=c[idx]; gap=max(M-topc.min(), M*0.2,1e-300)
       w=np.exp((topc-M)/gap); w/=w.sum()
       G=fft_gradient_weights(a,idx,w)
       # high/low
       high_idx=np.argsort(G)[::-1][:high_count*3]
       high_set=set()
       high=[]
       for h in high_idx:
          h=int(h)
          if a[h] > 1e-14 and h not in high_set:
             high.append(h); high_set.add(h)
             if len(high)>=high_count: break
       low_idx=np.argsort(G)[:low_count*2]
       low=[]
       for l in low_idx:
          l=int(l)
          if l not in high_set and len(low)<low_count: low.append(l)
       improved=False
       pairs_done=0
       for hi in high:
          if pairs_done>=max_pairs: break
          amt=a[hi]
          if amt <=1e-14: continue
          for lo in low:
             if pairs_done>=max_pairs: break
             pairs_done +=1
             best_pair_sc=sc
             best_pair_a=None
             base=a.copy()  # too many copies? For each pair, copy for each fraction. Could optimize but okay.
             for frac in fractions:
                delta=amt*frac
                if delta <=1e-18: continue
                cand=base.copy()
                cand[hi]-=delta
                if cand[hi] < -1e-15: continue
                cand[lo]+=delta
                cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
                ssum=cand.sum()
                if ssum<=0: continue
                cand/=ssum
                cand_sc=score_fft(cand)
                if cand_sc < best_pair_sc - 1e-13:
                   best_pair_sc=cand_sc; best_pair_a=cand
             if best_pair_a is not None:
                a=best_pair_a; improved=True; stall=0; break
          if improved: break
       if not improved:
          # random move if stall
          if stall>50:
             # choose random high i and random low j
             for _ in range(10):
                hi=int(rng.integers(n)); lo=int(rng.integers(n)); if hi==lo: continue
                if a[hi]<=1e-14: continue
                frac=float(rng.uniform(0.03,0.25)); delta=a[hi]*frac
                cand=a.copy(); cand[hi]-=delta; cand[lo]+=delta; cand=np.maximum(cand,0); s=...; if improve ... break
             if not improved: stall+=1
          else:
             stall+=1
       if verbose and it%... print? Need it variable.
    return best,best_sc
```
Add `it` increment for prints. In no-improved random moves, if improved break; else stall. Good.

Need avoid too many copies: `base=a.copy()` inside pair; then `cand=base.copy()` for fractions. Could do `cand=a.copy()` each fraction. Fine.

### `lp_fw_optimize` code details
```
def build_lp_matrix(a, active_list, n):
   K=len(active_list); coeffs=np.zeros((K,n),float)
   for r,k in enumerate(active_list):
      k=int(k)
      start= k - (n-1); if start<0: start=0
      end= k+1; if end>n: end=n
      if start<end:
         idx=np.arange(start,end)
         coeffs[r, idx]=a[k-idx]
   return coeffs
```

`lp_fw_optimize`:
```
def lp_fw_optimize(a0, time_limit, solver=None, init_K=120, max_K=300, add_per=15, verbose=True):
   if solver is None: solver=get_solver()
   if solver is None: return normalize_sequence(a0), score_fft(a0)
   y=normalize_sequence(a0); y=np.maximum(y,1e-15); y/=y.sum(); n=len(y)
   c=fast_convolution(y); M=c.max(); sc=2nM; best=y.copy(); best_sc=sc
   L=2*n-1; K0=min(init_K,L)
   idx=np.argpartition(c,K0-1)[:K0]
   active=set(int(k) for k in idx)
   t0=time.monotonic(); no_improve=0; it=0
   while time < limit:
      it+=1
      c=fast_convolution(y); M=c.max(); sc=2nM
      if sc < best_sc -1e-12: best... no_improve=0 else no_improve++
      if no_improve>800: break
      target_K=min(max_K, K0 + it*2)
      # add top target
      if target_K < L:
          top=np.argpartition(c,target_K-1)[:target_K]
      else: top=np.arange(L)
      for k in top: active.add(int(k))
      if len(active)>max_K:
          al=sorted(active)
          vals=c[al]
          keep=np.argsort(vals)[-max_K:]
          active=set(int(al[i]) for i in keep)
      active.add(int(np.argmax(c)))
      clist=sorted(active); K=len(clist)
      if K<2: continue
      try:
         coeffs=build_lp_matrix(y, clist, n)
         gvar=cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
         mvar=cp.Variable(nonneg=True)
         cons=[cp.sum(gvar)==1, cp.multiply? no, coeffs @ gvar <= mvar]
         prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(mvar), cons)
         prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
         if prob.status not in (cp.OPTIMAL, cp.OPTIMAL_INACCURATE): raise
      except Exception:
         # shrink active
         if len(active)>K0//2:
            al=sorted(active); vals=c[al]; keep=np.argsort(vals)[-K0//2:]; active=set(...)
         continue
      graw=np.asarray(gvar.value, dtype=float)
      g=np.maximum(graw,0); s=g.sum(); if s<=0: continue; g=g/s
      # line search
      best_alpha=1.0/(it+2); best_trial=sc; best_trial_y=None
      alphas=[best_alpha,0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]
      for alpha in alphas:
         trial=(1-alpha)*y + alpha*g; trial=np.maximum(trial,1e-15); trial/=trial.sum()
         trial_sc=score_fft(trial)
         if trial_sc<best_trial-1e-14: ...
      if best_trial_y is not None:
         y=best_trial_y
         if best_trial<best_sc-1e-12: best...
      # violations
      conv_yg=fast_cross_convolution(y, g) # before y update? use old? use current y and g
      viol=conv_yg > M + 1e-12
      if np.any(viol):
         vio_idx=np.where(viol)[0]
         vio_amt=conv_yg[vio_idx]
         order=np.argsort(vio_amt)[::-1][:add_per]
         for k in vio_idx[order]: active.add(int(k))
      if verbose and it%20==0: print
   return best,best_sc
```
Need `cp.OPTIMAL` string? In cvxpy, `cp.OPTIMAL` exists. Use `prob.status in ('optimal','optimal_inaccurate')` to be safe.

Potential issue: `coeffs @ gvar` in cvxpy with dense KxN okay. `mvar` scalar variable nonneg. Constraint matrix-vector <= scalar broadcasts. Good.

If K=1, LP trivial g? Could be. Skip if K<5? Not necessary.

Memory: `coeffs @ gvar` creates expression. Fine.

`fast_cross_convolution(y,g)` maybe length. Good.

### `get_solver` code with small test
```
_cp_solver = None

def get_solver():
    global _cp_solver
    if _cp_solver is not None:
        return _cp_solver
    if not _HAS_CP:
        return None
    try:
        for s in (cp.CLARABEL, cp.PDLP, cp.SCS, cp.ECOS):
            try:
                 x=cp.Variable(10, nonneg=True)
                 prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x)==1, x<=1])
                 prob.solve(solver=s, verbose=False)
                 if prob.status in ('optimal','optimal_inaccurate'):
                     _cp_solver=s
                     print("  LP solver:", s)
                     return s
            except Exception:
                 pass
        _cp_solver=cp.ECOS
        return cp.ECOS
    except Exception:
        return None
```

def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
   start=time.monotonic()

```python
"""
Searches for a nonnegative step-function sequence minimizing

    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2

using an FFT-based multiplicative descent method, exact local pair-move
polishing, an optional cvxpy active-set Frank-Wolfe stage, and length
interpolation.  All internal objectives use the same full linear convolution
as the evaluator, but are evaluated by FFT for speed.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import cvxpy as cp
    _HAS_CP = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_CP = False

_cp_solver = None
_FFT_CONV_CACHE = {}
_FFT_GRAD_CACHE = {}

# Small positive mass used to keep mirror updates numerically safe.  Its
# contribution to the objective is far below the intended precision.
_MICRO = 1e-12


def get_fft_conv_size(n):
    v = _FFT_CONV_CACHE.get(n)
    if v is None:
        size = 2 * int(n) - 1
        v = 1 << (int(size) - 1).bit_length()
        _FFT_CONV_CACHE[n] = v
    return int(v)


def get_fft_grad_size(n):
    v = _FFT_GRAD_CACHE.get(n)
    if v is None:
        size = 3 * int(n) - 2
        v = 1 << (int(size) - 1).bit_length()
        _FFT_GRAD_CACHE[n] = v
    return int(v)


def fast_convolution(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0] * a[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    fft_size = get_fft_conv_size(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, fft_size)[:2 * n - 1]
    return c


def fast_cross_convolution(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0] * b[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    fft_size = get_fft_conv_size(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, fft_size)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, fft_size)[:2 * n - 1]


def score_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def normalize_sequence(a, floor=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1e300)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    a = a / s
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, floor)
        s2 = float(np.sum(a))
        if s2 > 1e-300:
            a = a / s2
    return a


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == new_n:
        return normalize_sequence(a)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(b))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    return b / s


def random_perturb(a, rng, rel_std=0.02):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = a.copy()
    if a.size:
        b = b + rng.standard_normal(a.size) * rel_std * b
        b = b + _MICRO
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(b))
        if s > 1e-300:
            b = b / s
    return b


def make_initial_templates(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    out = []
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    eps = 1e-12

    out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for p in (-0.5, -0.4, -0.3, -0.2, -0.1, 0.0, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5):
        out.append((x + eps) ** p)

    sqrt_left = (x + eps) ** (-0.5)
    sqrt_right = ((1.0 - x) + eps) ** (-0.5)

    for alpha in (0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 1.2, 1.6, 2.4):
        out.append(sqrt_left + alpha * sqrt_right)

    out.append(sqrt_left * (1.0 - 0.25 * x))
    out.append(sqrt_left * (1.0 - 0.5 * x))
    out.append(sqrt_left + 0.35 * ((1.0 - x) + eps) ** 0.25)

    for cpos, wden in ((0.25, 0.07), (0.5, 0.09), (0.75, 0.07)):
        bump = np.exp(-((x - cpos) ** 2) / (2.0 * wden))
        out.append(sqrt_left + 0.45 * bump)

    L = max(1, n // 3)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b[:L] = 1.0
    b[-L:] = 1.0
    out.append(b)

    L = max(1, int(0.45 * n))
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b[:L] = 1.0
    b[-L:] = 1.0
    out.append(b)

    good = []
    for s in out:
        try:
            s = normalize_sequence(s)
            if s.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(s)):
                good.append(s)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return good


def fft_gradient_weights(a, active, weights):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k weights[k] * (a*a)[k] with respect to a,
    ignoring the global factor 2, which is irrelevant after normalization.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros_like(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    W = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    active = np.asarray(active, dtype=np.int64)
    weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64)
    if active.size == 0:
        return np.zeros_like(a)
    W[active] = weights
    Wf = W[::-1]
    fft_size = get_fft_grad_size(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(Wf, fft_size)
    R = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, fft_size)
    return R[2 * n - 2:n - 2:-1]


def mirror_optimize(a0, time_limit, K0=45, Kmax=380, verbose=True):
    """
    Multiplicative descent for the max of the full linear convolution.
    At each step a soft active set of lags gives a pressure field; mass is
    multiplicatively moved from high-pressure locations to low-pressure
    locations, followed by exact FFT line search.
    """
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a.size == 0 or time_limit <= 0:
        return a, score_fft(a)

    a = np.maximum(a, _MICRO)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones_like(a)
    else:
        a = a / s
    n = int(a.size)

    c = fast_convolution(a)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    best_sc = 2.0 * n * M
    best = a.copy()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    horizon = t0 + float(time_limit)
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    step_list = (0.25, 0.7, 1.6)

    while time.monotonic() < horizon:
        it += 1
        c = fast_convolution(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            break
        sc = 2.0 * n * M

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        if stall > 4000:
            break

        L = int(c.size)
        target_K = min(int(Kmax), int(K0) + it // 12)
        if stall > 500:
            target_K = max(18, min(target_K, 130))
        if stall > 1600:
            target_K = 18
        K = max(1, min(target_K, L))

        if K == L:
            idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, K - 1)[:K]

        topc = c[idx]
        tmin = float(np.min(topc)) if topc.size else M
        gap = max(M - tmin, 0.05 * M, 1e-300)
        if stall > 400:
            gap *= 0.6
        ex = np.exp((topc - M) / gap)
        sw = float(np.sum(ex))
        if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones(len(idx), dtype=np.float64) / float(len(idx))
        else:
            w = ex / sw

        G = fft_gradient_weights(a, idx, w)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
            a = best.copy()
            stall = 0
            continue

        gmean = float(np.mean(G))
        gmin = float(np.min(G))
        gmax = float(np.max(G))
        grange = gmax - gmin
        if grange < 1e-300:
            if stall > 50:
                rng = np.random.default_rng(98245 + stall)
                a = random_perturb(best, rng, 0.02 + 0.005 * min(3, stall // 800))
                stall = 0
            else:
                break
            continue

        u = (G - gmean) / grange
        base_log = np.log(a + _MICRO)

        best_trial = sc
        best_trial_a = None
        for step in step_list:
            z = base_log - step * u
            z -= float(np.max(z))
            cand = np.exp(z)
            cand = cand + _MICRO
            ssum = float(np.sum(cand))
            if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 1e-300:
                continue
            cand = cand / ssum
            trial_sc = score_fft(cand)
            if np.isfinite(trial_sc) and trial_sc < best_trial - 1e-14:
                best_trial = trial_sc
                best_trial_a = cand

        if best_trial_a is not None:
            if best_trial < sc - 1e-13:
                a = best_trial_a
                if best_trial < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = best_trial
                    best = a.copy()
                    stall = 0
            else:
                a = best.copy()
                if stall > 100:
                    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345 + stall)
                    a = random_perturb(best, rng, 0.02 + 0.007 * min(4, stall // 700))
                    stall = 0
        else:
            a = best.copy()
            if stall > 80:
                rng = np.random.default_rng(23456 + stall)
                a = random_perturb(best, rng, 0.02 + 0.007 * min(4, stall // 700))
                stall = 0

        if verbose and it % 250 == 0:
            print("    mirror it={:6d} t={:7.1f}s score={:.7f} best={:.7f} stall={}".format(
                it, time.monotonic() - t0, sc, best_sc, stall))

    return best, best_sc


def coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, high_count=16, low_count=28,
                      max_pairs=24, verbose=False):
    """
    Exact local polishing by sum-preserving transfers of mass from
    high-pressure coordinates to low-pressure coordinates.
    Candidate sequences are scored by full FFT convolution.
    """
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a.size == 0 or time_limit <= 0:
        return a, score_fft(a)
    a = np.maximum(a, _MICRO)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones_like(a)
    else:
        a = a / s
    n = int(a.size)

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_fft(best)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    horizon = t0 + float(time_limit)
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(505050)
    fractions = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.30, 0.50)

    while time.monotonic() < horizon:
        it += 1
        sc = score_fft(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        if stall > 3500:
            break

        c = fast_convolution(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        M = float(np.max(c))
        L = int(c.size)
        K = min(90, L)
        if K <= 0:
            continue
        if K == L:
            idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, K - 1)[:K]

        topc = c[idx]
        tmin = float(np.min(topc)) if topc.size else M
        gap = max(M - tmin, 0.20 * M, 1e-300)
        ex = np.exp((topc - M) / gap)
        sw = float(np.sum(ex))
        if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones(len(idx), dtype=np.float64) / float(len(idx))
        else:
            w = ex / sw

        G = fft_gradient_weights(a, idx, w)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
            stall += 1
            continue

        high_all = np.argsort(G)[::-1][:max(1, 3 * int(high_count))]
        high = []
        high_set = set()
        for hh in high_all:
            hh = int(hh)
            if a[hh] > 1e-14 and hh not in high_set:
                high.append(hh)
                high_set.add(hh)
                if len(high) >= int(high_count):
                    break

        low_all = np.argsort(G)[:max(1, 2 * int(low_count))]
        low = []
        for ll in low_all:
            ll = int(ll)
            if ll not in high_set:
                low.append(ll)
                if len(low) >= int(low_count):
                    break

        if len(high) == 0 or len(low) == 0:
            stall += 1
            continue

        improved = False
        pairs_done = 0
        for hi in high:
            if pairs_done >= int(max_pairs):
                break
            amt = float(a[hi])
            if amt <= 1e-14:
                continue
            for lo in low:
                if pairs_done >= int(max_pairs):
                    break
                if lo == hi:
                    continue
                pairs_done += 1

                pair_best_sc = sc
                pair_best_a = None
                for frac in fractions:
                    delta = amt * frac
                    if delta <= 1e-18:
                        continue
                    cand = a.copy()
                    cand[hi] -= delta
                    if cand[hi] < -1e-14:
                        continue
                    cand[lo] += delta
                    cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                    ss = float(np.sum(cand))
                    if ss <= 1e-300:
                        continue
                    cand = cand / ss
                    cand_sc = score_fft(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(cand_sc) and cand_sc < pair_best_sc - 1e-13:
                        pair_best_sc = cand_sc
                        pair_best_a = cand

                if pair_best_a is not None:
                    a = pair_best_a
                    improved = True
                    stall = 0
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if not improved:
            if stall > 60:
                did_random = False
                for _ in range(12):
                    hi = int(rng.integers(n))
                    lo = int(rng.integers(n))
                    if hi == lo:
                        continue
                    amt = float(a[hi])
                    if amt <= 1e-14:
                        continue
                    frac = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.30))
                    delta = amt * frac
                    cand = a.copy()
                    cand[hi] -= delta
                    cand[lo] += delta
                    cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                    ss = float(np.sum(cand))
                    if ss <= 1e-300:
                        continue
                    cand = cand / ss
                    cand_sc = score_fft(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(cand_sc) and cand_sc < sc - 1e-14:
                        a = cand
                        improved = True
                        stall = 0
                        did_random = True
                        break
                if not did_random:
                    stall += 1
            else:
                stall += 1

        if verbose and it % 250 == 0:
            print("    coord  it={:6d} t={:7.1f}s score={:.7f} best={:.7f} stall={}".format(
                it, time.monotonic() - t0, score_fft(a), best_sc, stall))

    return best, best_sc


def get_solver():
    global _cp_solver
    if _cp_solver is not None:
        return _cp_solver
    if not _HAS_CP:
        return None
    for s in (cp.CLARABEL, cp.PDLP, cp.SCS, cp.ECOS):
        try:
            x = cp.Variable(10, nonneg=True)
            prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x) == 1, x <= 1])
            prob.solve(solver=s, verbose=False)
            if prob.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
                _cp_solver = s
                print("    LP solver selected:", s)
                return _cp_solver
        except Exception:
            pass
    try:
        _cp_solver = cp.ECOS
        print("    LP solver selected: ECOS fallback")
        return _cp_solver
    except Exception:
        return None


def build_lp_matrix(a, active_list, n):
    K = len(active_list)
    coeffs = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(K):
        k = int(active_list[r])
        start = k - (n - 1)
        if start < 0:
            start = 0
        end = k + 1
        if end > n:
            end = n
        if start < end:
            idx = np.arange(start, end, dtype=np.int64)
            coeffs[r, idx] = a[k - idx]
    return coeffs


def lp_fw_optimize(a0, time_limit, solver=None, init_K=120, max_K=280,
                   add_per=15, verbose=True):
    """
    Active-set linearized Frank-Wolfe stage.  It solves a dense LP over a
    growing set of active convolution constraints, then line-searches between
    the current point and the LP direction.
    """
    if solver is None:
        solver = get_solver()
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a.size == 0 or time_limit <= 0:
        return a, score_fft(a)
    if solver is None:
        return a, score_fft(a)

    y = np.maximum(a, _MICRO)
    s = float(np.sum(y))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        y = np.ones_like(a)
    else:
        y = y / s
    n = int(y.size)

    c = fast_convolution(y)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    best_sc = 2.0 * n * M
    best = y.copy()

    L = int(c.size)
    K0 = max(1, min(int(init_K), L))
    if K0 == L:
        idx0 = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx0 = np.argpartition(c, K0 - 1)[:K0]
    active = set()
    for k in idx0:
        active.add(int(k))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    horizon = t0 + float(time_limit)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < horizon:
        it += 1

        c = fast_convolution(y)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            break
        sc = 2.0 * n * M

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best = y.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 900:
            break

        L = int(c.size)
        target_K = min(int(max_K), K0 + 2 * it)
        if target_K < L:
            top = np.argpartition(c, target_K - 1)[:target_K]
        else:
            top = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
        for k in top:
            active.add(int(k))
        active.add(int(np.argmax(c)))

        if len(active) > int(max_K):
            al = sorted(active)
            vals = c[al]
            keep = np.argsort(vals)[-int(max_K):]
            active = set()
            for kk in keep:
                active.add(int(al[int(kk)]))

        clist = sorted(active)
        if len(clist) < 2:
            continue

        try:
            coeffs = build_lp_matrix(y, clist, n)
            gvar = cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
            mvar = cp.Variable(nonneg=True)
            cons = [
                cp.sum(gvar) == 1,
                coeffs @ gvar <= mvar
            ]
            prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(mvar), cons)
            prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
            if prob.status not in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
                raise RuntimeError("LP status: " + str(prob.status))
            if gvar.value is None:
                raise RuntimeError("LP returned None")
        except Exception:
            if len(active) > max(10, K0 // 2):
                al = sorted(active)
                vals = c[al]
                keep = np.argsort(vals)[-max(10, K0 // 2):]
                active = set()
                for kk in keep:
                    active.add(int(al[int(kk)]))
            continue

        graw = np.asarray(gvar.value, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(graw)):
            continue
        g = np.maximum(graw, 0.0)
        gs = float(np.sum(g))
        if gs <= 1e-300:
            continue
        g = g / gs

        best_trial = sc
        best_trial_y = None
        for alpha in (1.0 / (it + 2.0), 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            trial = (1.0 - float(alpha)) * y + float(alpha) * g
            trial = np.maximum(trial, _MICRO)
            ts = float(np.sum(trial))
            if ts <= 1e-300:
                continue
            trial = trial / ts
            trial_sc = score_fft(trial)
            if np.isfinite(trial_sc) and trial_sc < best_trial - 1e-14:
                best_trial = trial_sc
                best_trial_y = trial

        if best_trial_y is not None:
            y = best_trial_y
            if best_trial < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = best_trial
                best = y.copy()

        try:
            conv_yg = fast_cross_convolution(y, g)
            viol = conv_yg > M + 1e-12
            if np.any(viol):
                vio_idx = np.where(viol)[0]
                vio_amt = conv_yg[vio_idx]
                order = np.argsort(vio_amt)[::-1][:int(add_per)]
                for kk in vio_idx[order]:
                    active.add(int(kk))
        except Exception:
            pass

        if verbose and it % 25 == 0:
            print("    LP-FW  it={:6d} t={:7.1f}s score={:.7f} best={:.7f} K={} noimp={}".format(
                it, time.monotonic() - t0, sc, best_sc, len(active), no_improve))

    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """
    Main entry point.  Returns a Python list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    horizon = start + max(120.0, float(budget_s) - 150.0)
    MAX_N = 20000

    seeds = []
    seq_id = 0

    parent_raw = None
    try:
        parent_raw = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        parent_raw = None

    if parent_raw is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(parent_raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
            if p.size > 10 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)) and float(np.sum(p)) > 0.0:
                if p.size > MAX_N:
                    p = interpolate_sequence(p, MAX_N)
                p = normalize_sequence(p)
                if p.size > 0:
                    sc = score_fft(p)
                    if np.isfinite(sc):
                        seeds.append((sc, seq_id, int(p.size), p, "parent"))
                        seq_id += 1
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_a = None

    if len(seeds) > 0:
        seeds.sort()
        best_score = float(seeds[0][0])
        best_a = seeds[0][3].copy()

    if len(seeds) == 0:
        n_tpl = 11700
    else:
        n_tpl = int(best_a.size) if best_a is not None else 11700
        if n_tpl > MAX_N:
            n_tpl = MAX_N
        if n_tpl < 8000:
            n_tpl = 8000

    if len(seeds) == 0 or best_score > 1.515:
        for t in make_initial_templates(n_tpl):
            sc = score_fft(t)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                seeds.append((sc, seq_id, int(t.size), t, "template"))
                seq_id += 1
        if len(seeds) > 0:
            seeds.sort()
            if seeds[0][0] < best_score:
                best_score = float(seeds[0][0])
                best_a = seeds[0][3].copy()

    if best_a is None:
        n_f = 10000
        best_a = np.ones(n_f, dtype=np.float64)
        best_a = best_a / float(n_f)
        best_score = score_fft(best_a)

    print("Initial candidate: n={} score={}".format(int(best_a.size), repr(float(best_score))))

    # Phase 1: main multiplicative refinement of the strongest seed.
    rem = horizon - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0 and best_a is not None:
        t_mirror = min(rem * 0.45, 720.0)
        if t_mirror > 20.0:
            out, sc_out = mirror_optimize(best_a, t_mirror, K0=45, Kmax=380, verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_out)
                best_a = out
                print("  mirror improved to {}".format(repr(float(best_score))))

    # Phase 2: exact local polishing.
    rem = horizon - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0 and best_a is not None:
        t_coord = min(rem * 0.17, 240.0)
        if t_coord > 15.0:
            out, sc_out = coordinate_polish(best_a, t_coord,
                                            high_count=16,
                                            low_count=28,
                                            max_pairs=24,
                                            verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_out)
                best_a = out
                print("  coord improved to {}".format(repr(float(best_score))))

    # Phase 3: optional active-set LP-FW polish for shorter sequences.
    rem = horizon - time.monotonic()
    if _HAS_CP and rem > 300.0 and best_a is not None and best_a.size <= 15000:
        solver = get_solver()
        if solver is not None:
            t_lp = min(rem * 0.23, 380.0)
            if t_lp > 60.0:
                out, sc_out = lp_fw_optimize(best_a, t_lp, solver=solver,
                                             init_K=120, max_K=280,
                                             add_per=15, verbose=True)
                if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc_out)
                    best_a = out
                    print("  LP-FW improved to {}".format(repr(float(best_score))))

    rem = horizon - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 40.0 and best_a is not None:
        t_coord2 = min(rem * 0.20, 200.0)
        if t_coord2 > 15.0:
            out, sc_out = coordinate_polish(best_a, t_coord2,
                                            high_count=18,
                                            low_count=30,
                                            max_pairs=30,
                                            verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_out)
                best_a = out
                print("  coord2 improved to {}".format(repr(float(best_score))))

    # Phase 4: length extension, if promising.
    rem = horizon - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 420.0 and best_a is not None and best_a.size < MAX_N:
        n2 = min(MAX_N, best_a.size + max(5000, best_a.size // 2))
        if n2 > best_a.size + 200:
            a2 = interpolate_sequence(best_a, n2)
            sc2 = score_fft(a2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2):
                if sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc2)
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    print("  length interpolation improved to {}".format(repr(float(best_score))))
                elif sc2 < best_score + 0.008:
                    base2 = a2
                    t1 = min(rem * 0.38, 330.0)
                    out2, scm = mirror_optimize(base2, t1, K0=50, Kmax=320, verbose=True)
                    if np.isfinite(scm) and scm < best_score:
                        best_score = float(scm)
                        best_a = out2
                        print("  long mirror improved to {}".format(repr(float(best_score))))
                    rem = horizon - time.monotonic()
                    t2 = min(rem * 0.30, 180.0)
                    if t2 > 20.0:
                        out3, scp = coordinate_polish(best_a, t2,
                                                      high_count=16,
                                                      low_count=28,
                                                      max_pairs=24,
                                                      verbose=True)
                        if np.isfinite(scp) and scp < best_score:
                            best_score = float(scp)
                            best_a = out3
                            print("  long coord improved to {}".format(repr(float(best_score))))

    # Phase 5: try a second nearby seed if time remains.
    rem = horizon - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 260.0 and len(seeds) > 1:
        sec = seeds[1]
        sc_sec = float(sec[0])
        if sc_sec < best_score + 0.035:
            a_sec = sec[3].copy()
            t_m = min(rem * 0.55, 180.0)
            out, sc_out = mirror_optimize(a_sec, t_m, K0=45, Kmax=320, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_out)
                best_a = out
                print("  second seed improved to {}".format(repr(float(best_score))))
            rem = horizon - time.monotonic()
            t_c = min(rem * 0.50, 90.0)
            if t_c > 20.0:
                out2, sc_out2 = coordinate_polish(best_a, t_c,
                                                  high_count=14,
                                                  low_count=24,
                                                  max_pairs=20,
                                                  verbose=False)
                if np.isfinite(sc_out2) and sc_out2 < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc_out2)
                    best_a = out2
                    print("  second coord improved to {}".format(repr(float(best_score))))

    # Final polish with all remaining time.
    rem = horizon - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0 and best_a is not None:
        t_final = min(rem * 0.85, 300.0)
        if t_final > 10.0:
            out, sc_out = coordinate_polish(best_a, t_final,
                                            high_count=20,
                                            low_count=32,
                                            max_pairs=36,
                                            verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_out)
                best_a = out
                print("  final coord improved to {}".format(repr(float(best_score))))

    rem = horizon - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0 and best_a is not None:
        t_final2 = min(rem * 0.80, 180.0)
        if t_final2 > 30.0:
            out, sc_out = mirror_optimize(best_a, t_final2, K0=35, Kmax=250, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_out)
                best_a = out
                print("  final mirror improved to {}".format(repr(float(best_score))))
            rem = horizon - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 40.0:
                out2, sc_out2 = coordinate_polish(best_a, min(rem * 0.8, 120.0),
                                                  high_count=16,
                                                  low_count=26,
                                                  max_pairs=24,
                                                  verbose=False)
                if np.isfinite(sc_out2) and sc_out2 < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc_out2)
                    best_a = out2
                    print("  final mirror+coord improved to {}".format(repr(float(best_score))))

    # Sanitize the output.  Normalizing to sum 1 is harmless because the
    # evaluator's objective is scale-invariant before its upper clipping,
    # and all returned values are far below 1000.
    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if best_a.size == 0:
        best_a = np.ones(10000, dtype=np.float64)
        best_a = best_a / 10000.0
    else:
        best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(np.sum(best_a))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
            best_a = np.ones(10000, dtype=np.float64)
            best_a = best_a / 10000.0
        else:
            best_a = best_a / s

    final_fft_score = score_fft(best_a)
    print("FINAL: n={} internal_fft_score={} elapsed={:.1f}s".format(
        int(best_a.size), repr(float(final_fft_score)), time.monotonic() - start))

    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
